### Convolution and score
Use precomputed plan per length. To reduce allocation, a function returns c maybe. For line search many, we can compute score only: `peak_conv(a)`. Use real FFT.
Potential issue: `scipy.fft.rfft` with `overwrite_x=True` to save? We can use `fft`? Let's implement:
```
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
class ConvPlanner:
  def __init__(self,n): self.n=n; self.m=2*n-1; self.N=next_fast_len(self.m)
  def cc(self,a):
     # ensure float64, length n
     A=rfft(a, self.N)
     c=irfft(A*A, self.N)[:self.m]
     c[~np.isfinite(c)]=0
     return c
  def peak(self,a): return self.cc(a).max()
```
Can use `norm='forward'`? Not needed. For speed, `rfft(a, N)` copies. Could use `rfft(a, N, overwrite_x=True)` but if a reused no. For many calls, ok. Use `np.max(c)`.

For n small, FFT convolution may have small negative due roundoff; max nonnegative but we clip. We can use `np.maximum(c,0)`, but max not affected if negatives. For exact evaluator uses direct; small errors may cause score slightly different. Use exact `evaluate_sequence` for final. For internal best, maybe our FFT score; could overestimate/underestimate by ~1e-12. Fine.

```

```

Could use local pairwise swap search based on exact active set to force improvement. Since scores target close, need high precision. Pairwise transfer can be very effective on simplex. Let's design a coordinate pairwise optimizer.

Given a, want small transfer δ from i to j. The change in convolution coefficients is quadratic:
For move δ from i to j: b = a + δ(v_j - v_i), where v_k is unit.
New c_k = c_k + 2δ (a_cross_k) + δ^2 d_k, where a_cross = a*v_j - a*v_i? Convolution with d: c(a+d)=c+ c(a,d)+c(a,d)? Since c is convolution, c(b)=c + 2(a conv d) + d conv d. d has -δ at i, +δ at j. a conv d = δ( a shifted j - a shifted i). d conv d = δ^2( e_i*e_i - e_i*e_j - e_j*e_i + e_j*e_j ), with coefficients positive at 2i? Let's use full convolution. We can compute `vec_change(t)` if needed.

```
def exact_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    if a.size==0 or not finite or sum<0.01: return inf
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    return float(2*a.size*np.max(b)/np.sum(a)**2)
```
This mirrors evaluator. We can use for candidate updates periodically, especially when internal improves significantly. But since FFT score is accurate, we can just final. To avoid O(n^2) too often, use every 50 improvements.

#### Initialization generation function
```
def make_cand(arr, target_n):
   arr=np.asarray(arr,float).clip(0,1000)
   if arr.size != target_n:
      if arr.size == 0: arr=np.ones(target_n)
      # linear interpolation from centers to centers
      old=np.asarray(arr)
      s=old.sum(); old=old/s if s>0 else np.ones...
      old_x=(np.arange(len(old))+0.5)/len(old)
      new_x=(np.arange(target_n)+0.5)/target_n
      old = np.interp(new_x, old_x, old)
   old=np.clip(old,0,1000)
   sm=old.sum()
   if sm<1e-12: old=np.ones(target_n)/target_n; sm=1
   return old/sm
```
For parent, N=192 exact. For other N, interpolate normalized parent. Note parent likely length 192 sum not 1? We normalize.

```
def power_cand(n,a,b):

   vals=np.clip(vals,0,1000)
   sm=vals.sum(); if sm<1e-18: return uniform mid? maybe
   return vals/sm
```

### LP function details
```
def lp_improve(a, conn, deadline, max_iters=..., verbose=False):
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=conn.score(a); improved=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       c0=conn.conv(a); M=c0.max()
       # Build B
       n=conn.n; m=2*n-1
       B=np.zeros((m,n))
       for j in range(n): B[j:j+n,j]=a
       # Peak-min LP
       c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1
       A_ub=B; # need matrix with p col
       A_ub_full = np.hstack((B, -np.ones(m))? row: B@x - p <=0 -> [B, -1]
       b_ub=np.zeros(m)
       A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1
       b_eq=[1.0]
       bounds=[(0,None)]*n + [(0,None)]
       res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub_full, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
       if fail: break
       g=np.clip(res.x[:n],0,None); gs=g.sum(); if gs<... break
       g/=gs
       # cross and cg
       cross=conn.conv(a,g) # but cross for b=(1-t)a+tg? Need a*g convolution with a and g. conn.conv multiplies two? Our conn.conv currently squares. Need cross_conv.
```
We need cross convolution of two arrays. Implement in conn:
```
def conv2(u,v): return irfft(rfft(u,N)*rfft(v,N),N)[:m]
def conv(a): conv2(a,a)
```
Then `cross=conv2(a,g)`. Blend: c = (1-t)^2 c0 + 2t(1-t) cross + t^2 cg. Here cross = a*g, cg=g*g.
```
       cg=conn.conv(g)
       # evaluate blend t
       def peak_blend(t):
           return np.max((1-t)**2*c0 + 2*t*(1-t)*cross + t*t*cg)
       # grid
       ts=np.linspace(0,1,21)
       vals=...
       tbest=...
       # refine
       lo=max(0,tbest-step); hi=min(1,tbest+step); ts2...
       h=(1-tbest)*a+tbest*g
       hsc=2*n*max_blend? Use conn.score blended via arrays (avoid conv FFT). Since precomputed exact polynomial.
       if hsc < best_sc - 1e-12: a=h; best_a=h; best_sc=hsc
       else: maybe try g from max-sum? or perturb.
       if improvement small for several >: break
```

### Pair phase details
We need `pair_phase(a, conn, deadline, rng, min_improvement=1e-12)`.
Implementation steps pseudo:
```
def pair_phase(a, conn, deadline, max_iters=100000, seed):
   n=conn.n; m=2n-1
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=conn.score(a)
   c=conn.conv(best)
   # helper exact_path
   stuck=0
   recompute_weights=0
   donors=recipients=None
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time > deadline: break
      if recompute_weights or donors None:
         M=c.max()
         # weights: top fraction maybe 0.2 or smooth
         # choose mode
         if rng.random()<0.5:
            thr = M - 0.2*(M - c.min())
            idx=c>=thr; w=... maybe equal weight among idx, if all one use smooth? 
         else:
            tau=max(1e-12,0.05*M); w=softmax((c-M)/tau)
         h=grad_h(best,w,conn)
         hnorm = h - h.min() maybe.
         # donors top 25 of h, recipients bottom 25
         hperm = np.argsort(h)
         L=max(2,n//4); donors=hperm[-L:]; recipients=hperm[:L]
         recompute=20
      i=int(rng.choice(donors)); j=int(rng.choice(recipients))
      if i==j: continue
      # Try move from i to j with t up to best[i]
      hi=best[i]
      if hi<1e-15: continue
      Larr=np.zeros(m); Larr[j:j+n]=best; Larr[i:i+n]-=best; Larr*=2
      p0=2*i; p1=i+j; p2=2*j
      # find best t
      def val(t):
         y=c + t*Larr
         t2=t*t
         if 0<=p0<m: y[p0]+=t2
         if 0<=p1<m: y[p1]-=2*t2
         if 0<=p2<m: y[p2]+=t2
         return np.max(y)
      # We can first evaluate few fractions
      fracs=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9,1.0)
      best_t=0; best_v=c.max()
      for f in fracs: t=f*hi; v=val(t); if v<best_v-1e-15: best_v=v; best_t=t
      if best_t>0:
         # refine around best fraction +/- step (if not at endpoints)
         lo=max(0,best_t-hi*0.1); hi2=min(hi,best_t+hi*0.1)
         ts=np.linspace(lo,hi2,11)
         for t in ts: v=val(t) ...
      if best_t>0 and best_v < best_sc/(2*n) - 1e-14:  # best_sc=2n*peak
          # apply update
          best = best.copy(); best[i]-=best_t; best[j]+=best_t
          # ensure nonneg
          best = np.maximum(best,0); s=best.sum(); best/=s; # after move sum unchanged if t exact, but floating
          c=c + best_t*Larr + best_t*best_t*Q? Better recompute from scratch to avoid error after norm:
          c=conn.conv(best)
          cur_sc=2*n*c.max()
          if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
              best_sc=cur_sc; best_a? update global? stuck=0
              # maybe recompute h sooner
              recompute=5
          else:
              stuck += ...
      else:
         stuck +=1
         if stuck > 1000: break
```

### Exact score function vs internal
We can copy evaluator exact but use `np.convolve` for n up to 320. It also clips negative and inf. We can use for best updates:
```
def exact_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size ==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return np.inf
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<0.01: return np.inf
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    mb=b.max()
    if not np.isfinite(mb): return np.inf
    return float(2*a.size*mb/(s*s))
```
Call exact for initial candidates? For sorting internal may differ negligible. To save, use internal for sorting; exact for updates after phase. But if internal score underestimates due FFT roundoff (could choose worse), exact final. We can in optimize function after each phase compute exact and compare. Also at end exact compare all top candidates.

### ConnPlan and grad_h
Implement class outside? We can define within propose or global. Top-level functions allowed. But `conn` needs n.

```
class ConvPlan:
    def __init__(self,n):
       self.n=int(n); self.m=2*n-1; self.N=next_fast_len(self.m)
       self.Ng=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
    def conv_sq(self,a):
       A=rfft(a, self.N)
       c=irfft(A*A, self.N)[:self.m]
       return c
    def conv2(self,u,v):
       U=rfft(u,self.N); V=rfft(v,self.N); c=irfft(U*V,self.N)[:self.m]; return c
    def score(self,a):
       c=self.conv_sq(a)
       # maybe c[~finite]=0
       return float(2*self.n*(c.max()))
    def h(self,a,w):
       Wr=w[::-1]
       A=rfft(a,self.Ng); W=rfft(Wr,self.Ng); conv=irfft(A*W,self.Ng).real
       return conv[self.n-1:self.m][::-1]
```
For `score`, with sum=1; if not, should scale? We'll normalize before. Use exact for final. If sum not 1, score would need divide by sum^2. But we'll always normalize.

`h` uses w length m. If w all finite. If w has zeros. Need `np.divide`. Use float.


### Potential bug in pair phase L shift signs
Let's verify with a small example. n=3, a=[a0,a1,a2]. Move mass from i=0 to j=2. d=[-t,0,t]. New b=[a0-t,a1,a2+t]. Conv b coefficients:
c0=(a0-t)^2=c0 -2t a0 + t^2.
Our L=2*(shift_j - shift_i) for t coefficient. shift_j length3 with a at j=2: L positions 2: a0,3:a1,4:a2. shift_i at i=0: positions 0:a0,1:a1,2:a2. For np.convolve [a] with itself, c1=2a0a1? Wait for n=3, c_k =∑ a_i a_{k-i}. k=1: i=0 a0a1, i=1 a1a0 =>2a0a1. b1: i=0 (a0-t)a1=(a0 -t)a1; i=1 a1*(a2+t)=a1a2 +t a1; i=2? a2? k-i negative. Total =a0a1 -t a1 + a1a2 +t a1 = a0a1+a1a2. Missing original? Original c1= a0a1 + a1a0? Actually a1a0 same term as a0a1, but convolution sum counts ordered pairs: i=0 and i=1 both. b c1: i=0 b0*b1 (a0-t)a1, i=1 b1*b0 a1(a0-t) = two terms! I forgot. Then c1 b =2[(a0-t)a1] +? Wait k=1, pairs (0,1) and (1,0) both valid because b0,b1. b2? no. So c1 =2(a0-t)a1 =2a0a1 -2t a1. Good L -2a1. ### LP cross and cg conv2
Cross = conv2(a,g) coefficient c_cross_k =∑ a_i g_{k-i}. Blend formula uses c0 + 2t(1-t) cross + t^2 cg because b=(1-t)a+t g. Conv b = (1-t)^2 a*a + t(1-t)(a*g) + t(1-t)(g*a)? ### Vectorized pair val optimization
We can avoid constructing Larr each pair by using c shifts? Could compute L0 and L1? For given pair, Larr = 2*(a shifted j - shifted i). We can create by slices. Copy c plus. Use `temp = c.copy(); temp[i:i+n] -= a*?; temp[j:j+n] += a*?`? Since L=2(s_j - s_i). For a given t, `y=c + 2t*(s_j - s_i)`. We can compute in-place from c copy each eval:
```
y=c.copy()
yt=2*t
y[j:j+n] += yt*a
y[i:i+n] -= yt*a
y[p0]+=t2; y[p1]-=2t2; y[p2]+=t2
return y.max()
```
This avoids prealloc Larr and multiplication `t*Larr`; but two slice adds per eval. With a length n, m 383, n 192, similar. For multiple evals, we could precompute `Aj = a` etc. It may be faster than Larr length m? Let's compare: Larr precompute one O(m), eval does `c + tL` O(m) one vector op. In-place per eval does two slice adds O(n) + Q O(1) plus copy m. So `c.copy()` + two slice adds maybe O(m+n) vs `c + tL` alloc+add O(m). Similar. Could precompute Larr once per pair; eval `c + t*Larr` uses optimized vector multiply+add allocate. Fine.

We can cache `a` as current list? np array.

```
def _lp_phase(a, plan, deadline, global_best, rng):
   current=a.copy(); best_loc=current; best_sc=exact? internal; stuck=0
   bounds_const...

      # peak min LP
      res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(m), A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
      if not res.success: maybe maxsum?

      if close current: stuck++
      cross=plan.conv2(current,g); cg=plan.conv_sq(g)
      t,best_peak = _goldenBlend(c0,cross,cg)
```

```
def _smooth_phase(a, plan, deadline, rng, global_best, base_iter=2000):
```

### Pair phase implementation details with local best and global
```
def _pair_phase(a, plan, deadline, rng, max_iters=200000, update_interval=50):
   current=a.copy(); best=current; best_sc=plan.score(current)
   c=plan.conv_sq(current)
   n=plan.n; m=plan.m
   fracs=(0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0)
   dono=None; reci=None; re=0; stuck=0
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
      if re<=0 or dono is None:
         c=plan.conv_sq(current)
         sc=2*n*c.max()
         if sc < best_sc - 1e-12: best_sc=sc; best=current.copy()
         M=c.max(); minc=c.min()
         if rng.random()<0.5:
            tau=max(1e-15,0.05*M)
            w=_softmax_weights(c,tau)
         else:
            # top 10% weighted by (c-M)^-? 
            order=np.argsort(c)[-max(10,n//10):]
            w=np.zeros(m)
            # weights inverse distance to max? 
            vals=max(c.max()-c,0); vals[order]=1
            # simpler equal
            w[order]=1/len(order)
         h=plan.h(current,w)
         hord=np.argsort(h)
         L=max(3,n//4)
         dono=hord[-L:].copy(); np.random.shuffle? use rng.permutation
         reci=hord[:L].copy()
         re=30
      re-=1
      i=int(rng.choice(dono)); j=int(rng.choice(reci))
      if i==j: continue
      hi=current[i]
      if hi <=1e-15: continue
      p0=2*i; p1=i+j; p2=2*j
      # precompute Larr
      Larr=np.zeros(m); Larr[j:j+n]=current; Larr[i:i+n]-=current; Larr*=2
      best_t=0; best_v=c.max()
      # quick check linear at small t? maybe use val
      for f in fracs:
         t=f*hi
         y=c + t*Larr
         t2=t*t
         if p0<m: y[p0]+=t2
         if p1<m: y[p1]-=2*t2
         if p2<m: y[p2]+=t2
         v=float(y.max())
         if v < best_v - 1e-15:
             best_v=v; best_t=t
             if f==1.0: break? not necessarily; max may interior
      if best_t>0:
         frac_best=best_t/hi
         lo=max(0.0, frac_best-0.12); hi2=min(1.0, frac_best+0.12)
         ts=np.linspace(lo,hi2,15)
         for frac in ts:
            t=frac*hi
            ...
      if best_t>0:
         # apply and recompute exact internal
         old=current.copy()
         current = old.copy()
         current[i]-=best_t; current[j]+=best_t
         # due float, if current[i]<0 small: set0
         if current[i] < 1e-300: current[i]=0.0
         s=current.sum(); if s>0: current/=s
         sc2=plan.score(current)
         if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best_sc=sc2; best=current.copy(); stuck=0
             c=plan.conv_sq(current) # for next vals? We can recompute c now; `c` will be used next iter but after normalization maybe changed. It's okay; but we can set c=plan.conv_sq(current) to ensure.
             re=10
             if update_count and self...: update global maybe
         else:
             current=old; stuck+=1
      else:
         stuck+=1
      if stuck > 5000: break
   return best,best_sc
```
Important: We use `c` as baseline for val. If we recompute c after accept, and in same loop next iter, okay. But if we recompute c before evaluating new pair, and also at top, need ensure baseline `c` corresponds to `current`. In above, after accept we set c=conv(current). Next iteration if re>0, use same c; good. At top if re<=0 recompute too. Fine.
If after trying pair, no accept, c unchanged. If we did recompute c earlier at top, matches current. Good.
Need ensure after normalizing current, c recomputed; if normalization factor ~1, okay.

The pair val uses Q additions at positions even if p1 duplicate? no. If p0,p1,p2 out of range? i,j in [0,n-1], p0<=2n-2=m-1; p2<=2n-2; p1<=2n-2. So always valid. No need if. But if i/j? valid. Use direct.

If moving t=hi (all mass from i to j), current[i]=0. Then normalization s unchanged (floating). good.

The pair search only moves from high h to low h. Could also try moving from low to high occasionally for exploration? Not needed.

What if donor high h has zero mass? Then skip. Donors list top h among all; may include zeros if h high at zeros (could happen due gradient). If hi small, skip. If too many skips, update donors. Fine.

### Exact update_best
In propose, maintain `best_score` and `best_arr`. Function:
```
def update_best(arr, force_exact=True):
    nonlocal best_score,best_arr
    arr=np.asarray(arr,float)
    if arr.size==0 or not finite: return
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<0.01: return
    arr=arr/s
    sc=exact_score(arr)
    if sc < best_score:
       best_score=sc; best_arr=arr.copy()
```

### Generate candidates within time
Let's write compact:
```
def generate(parent, lens, seed, deadline):
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   cands=[]; plan_cache={}
   def add(arr,name):
      arr=np.asarray(arr,float).ravel()
      if arr.size==0: return
      arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
      s=arr.sum()
      if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return
      arr=arr/s
      n=arr.size
      plan=plan_cache.setdefault(n, ConvPlan(n))
      sc=2*n*plan.conv_sq(arr).max()
      cands.append((sc, arr.copy(), n, name))
   # parent/resample
   for N in lens:
       if parent is not None:
          if parent.size==N: add(parent,'parent')
          else: add(np.interp((np.arange(N)+0.5)/N, (np.arange(parent.size)+0.5)/parent.size, parent), f'resample{N}')
       # power
       if time > deadline: break
       x=(np.arange(N)+0.5)/N
       for a_exp in [0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0]:
          for b_exp in [0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0]:
             v=np.where((X==0)&(a_exp==0),1,X**a_exp) * ...
             add(v,f'powerN{N}')
   # random log/fourier
   num= min(800, max(100, int((deadline-now)/0.05))) maybe 500
   for N in lens:
      x=...
      for trial in range(num):
         if time>deadline: break
         kind=rng.integers(0,3)
         if kind==0: # log polynomial
            z=rng.normal(0,1,3)
            f=np.exp(z[0]+z[1]*x+z[2]*x*x)
         elif kind==1: # cosine
            f=1+rng.normal(0,1)*np.cos(np.pi*x)+rng.normal(0,1)*np.sin(np.pi*x)+rng.normal(0,0.7)*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
         else:
            # product power with random exponents
            a=rng.uniform(0,3); b=rng.uniform(0,3); f=np.power(x,a)*(1-x)**b
         f=np.clip(f,1e-12,1000); add(f,...)
```
Need avoid too much. For x=0 and exp=0, `x**0` in numpy yields 1? 0**0 =1 with warning? It returns 1? Use `np.where(x==0, 1.0, x**a)` if a=0; but if a>0, 0**a=0 okay. Could use `np.power(X, a, where=X>0)`? Simpler: `f = X**a * (1-X)**b`; for a=0, X**0 all 1 including 0, numpy gives 1. Fine no warning? 0**0 is 1 in numpy? I think yes with RuntimeWarning? It may not. To be safe:
```
def pow_term(base, exp):
   if exp == 0: return np.ones_like(base)
   return np.where(base>0, base**exp, 0.0)
```

#### normalize_array
```
def normalize_array(a, require_len=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return None
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return None
    return a/s
```
If we multiply by 1000? no.

`resample`:
```
def resample_array(a,n):
   a=normalize_array(a)
   if a is None: return np.ones(n)/n
   if a.size==n: return a
   old_x=(np.arange(a.size)+0.5)/a.size
   new_x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   b=np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
   b=normalize_array(b)
   if b is None: return np.ones(n)/n
   return b
```

#### softmax_weights
```
def softmax_weights(c, tau):
   c=np.asarray(c)
   cm=c.max()
   x=np.clip((c-cm)/tau, -700.0, 0.0) # c-cm<=0; clip upper 0
   ex=np.exp(x); s=ex.sum()
   if not finite or s==0: w=zeros; w[argmax]=1
   return ex/s
```
If tau too small, ex for nonmax underflow to0, w one. Fine.

#### active_weights
```
def active_weights(c, frac=0.2):
   m=c.size; k=max(1, int(frac*m))
   idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:] if k<m else np.arange(m)
   w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=1/k; return w
```
Argpartition O(m). For m small. Could use top k by sort. Good.

#### LP Phase

`_blend_best` helper top-level:
```
def _best_blend(c0,cross,cg):
   ts=np.linspace(0,1,21)
   def vals(ts):
       one=1-ts
       arr = (one*one)[:,None]*c0[None,:] + (2*one*ts)[:,None]*cross[None,:] + (ts*ts)[:,None]*cg[None,:]
       return arr.max(axis=1)
   v=vals(ts); i=int(v.argmin()); t0=ts[i]
   # if i>0 and i<len-1: lo=ts[i-1]; hi=ts[i+1] else lo=0;hi=1
   ts2=np.linspace(lo,hi,25)
   v2=vals(ts2); j=int(v2.argmin()); return ts2[j], float(v2[j])
```
Memory: 25*383, fine.

LP phase building A each loop. We can precompute `A_eq`, `b_eq`, `c_obj`, etc.
```
def lp_phase(a, plan, deadline, rng):
   n=plan.n; m=2*n-1
   current=normalize_array(a); if None return a, inf
   best=current.copy(); best_sc=plan.score(current)
   c_obj=...
   maxsum_obj=-np.ones(n)
   bounds=...
   b_ub0=np.zeros(m)
   stuck=0
   for it in range(5000):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
      c0=plan.conv_sq(current)
      M=c0.max()
      if M<=0: break
      A=np.empty((m,n+1)); A[:, :n]=0.0; A[:,-1]=-1.0
      for j in range(n): A[j:j+n,j]=current
      try: res=linprog(...)
      except: break
      if not res.success:
          # try maxsum? maybe
          try: res2=linprog(maxsum_obj, A_ub=A[:,:n], b_ub=np.full(m,M), bounds=bounds_g, method='highs')
          if success and x sum...
          else break
      g=...
      if np.linalg.norm(g-current) < 1e-8: break
      cross=plan.conv2(current,g); cg=plan.conv_sq(g)
      t,bp=_best_blend(c0,cross,cg)
      if t>0:
         cand=(1-t)*current + t*g; cs=cand.sum(); if cs>0 cand/=cs
         sc=2*n*bp
         if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
             current=cand; best=cand; best_sc=sc; stuck=0
         else:
             # try maxsum if stuck
             stuck+=1
      else: stuck+=1
      if stuck>8: break
   return best.copy(), best_sc
```
Need `bounds_g = [(0,None)]*n` for maxsum. If peak-min fails but maxsum succeeds and improves? Include.
If `res.x` p may be 0? fine. We don't use p.
Potential high memory per iteration A allocation. ok.

#### Smooth phase
Need `plan.h`.
```
def smooth_phase(a, plan, deadline, rng):
   current=normalize_array(a); best=current.copy(); best_sc=plan.score(current)
   eta=0.1; stuck=0; last_best_it=0
   n=plan.n
   for it in range(20000):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      c=plan.conv_sq(current); sc=2*n*c.max()
      if sc < best_sc - 1e-13: best=current.copy(); best_sc=sc; stuck=0; last_best_it=it
      M=c.max(); minc=c.min()
      if rng.random()<0.7:
         tau=max(1e-15, 0.04*M)
         w=softmax_weights(c,tau)
      else:
         w=active_weights(c,0.25)
      h=plan.h(current,w)
      hm=h.max()
      if not finite or hm<1e-300: break
      hn=h/hm
      z=np.log(np.maximum(current,1e-300));
      # Mirror line search
      improved=False
      for e in (eta, 0.5*eta, 2*eta):
          z2=z - e*hn
          z2-=z2.max()
          cand=np.exp(z2); s=cand.sum(); if s<1e-30: continue; cand/=s
          csc=plan.score(cand)
          if csc < sc - 1e-13:
             current=cand; sc=csc; best=cand; best_sc=sc; eta=e; improved=True; break
      if not improved:
         # euclidean attempt (maybe one alpha)
         step=hn - np.dot(hn,current)
         sn=norm(step)
         if sn>1e-14:
            for alpha in (eta*0.1, eta*0.2):
               cand=current - alpha*step/sn
               cand=np.maximum(cand,0); s=cand.sum(); if s<1e-30: continue; cand/=s; csc=plan.score(cand)
               if csc < sc - 1e-13: ...
         if improved: stuck=0; eta=min(eta*1.05,2.0)
         else:
            stuck+=1; eta=max(eta*0.9,0.02)
            if stuck>200:
               # sometimes noice current to local best? We'll break to pair.
               break
            if stuck%25==0:
               # perturbs current but keep best
               current = normalize_array(current + rng.normal(0,0.01,current.size))
               if current is None: current=best.copy()
   return best.copy(), best_sc
```
Concern: Smooth phase `z` recomputed each iter; if current has tiny values (after mirror) log -700, okay. If current has exact zeros, log(1e-300) ~ -690; softmax may not represent zeros exactly but okay.
The Euclidean step `step=hn - dot(hn,current)` ensures sum zero because sum of hn? sum step = sum hn - sum current*sum hn = sum hn(1 - sum current)=0. Good.

#### Pair phase
Need `_try_pair` maybe inline. Use plan.n/m.
```
def pair_phase(a, plan, deadline, rng, max_iters=2000000):
   current=normalize_array(a); best=current.copy(); best_sc=plan.score(current); c=plan.conv_sq(current)
   n=plan.n; m=2*n-1
   fracs=(0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.15,0.2,0.3,0.4,0.5,0.65,0.8,1.0)
   dono=None; reci=None; re=0; stuck=0; improves=0
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      if re<=0 or dono is None:
         c=plan.conv_sq(current)
         sc=2*n*c.max()
         if sc < best_sc - 1e-12: best=current.copy(); best_sc=sc
         M=c.max(); minc=c.min()
         if rng.random()<0.55:
             w=softmax_weights(c, max(1e-15, 0.05*M))
         else:
             w=active_weights(c,0.2)
         h=plan.h(current,w)
         hord=np.argsort(h)
         L=max(4,n//4)
         dono=hord[-L:].copy(); reci=hord[:L].copy()
         # perhaps shuffle with rng
         dono=rng.permutation(dono); reci=rng.permutation(reci)
         re=40
      re-=1
      i=int(rng.choice(dono)); j=int(rng.choice(reci))
      if i==j: continue
      hi=float(current[i])
      if hi < 1e-15: continue
      Larr=np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
      Larr[j:j+n] = current
      Larr[i:i+n] -= current
      Larr *= 2.0
      p0=2*i; p1=i+j; p2=2*j
      base_max=c.max()
      best_t=0.0; best_v=base_max
      # initial scanning
      for f in fracs:
         t=f*hi
         y=c + t*Larr
         t2=t*t
         y[p0]+=t2; y[p1]-=2*t2; y[p2]+=t2
         v=float(y.max())
         if v < best_v - 1e-15:
            best_v=v; best_t=t
      if best_t > 0:
         frac=best_t/hi
         lo=max(0.0, frac-0.1); hi2=min(1.0, frac+0.1)
         if hi2>lo:
            for f in np.linspace(lo,hi2,13):
               if f==0: continue
               t=f*hi
               y=c + t*Larr
               t2=t*t
               y[p0]+=t2; y[p1]-=2*t2; y[p2]+=t2
               v=float(y.max())
               if v < best_v - 1e-15: best_v=v; best_t=t
      if best_t > 0:
         cand = current.copy(); cand[i]-=best_t; cand[j]+=best_t
         # if tiny negative from round:
         if cand[i] < 0: cand[i]=0.0
         s=cand.sum(); if s<=0: continue; cand/=s
         sc2=plan.score(cand)
         if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            current=cand; best=current.copy(); best_sc=sc2; c=plan.conv_sq(current); re=20; stuck=0; improves+=1
            if improves % 200 == 0: # could allow more? no
                pass
         else:
            stuck+=1
      else:
         stuck+=1
         if stuck % 4000 == 0 and stuck < 60000:
             # gently perturb current? Keep best? We can add small noise to current (not best) to escape? Pair only improvements from current; if current stuck, maybe reset to best plus noise.
             noised = normalize_array(best + rng.normal(0,0.005,best.size))
             if noised is not None:
                 scn=plan.score(noised)
                 if scn < best_sc + 1e-6: # allow slight worse? maybe
                     current=noised; c=plan.conv_sq(current)
                 else:
                     current=best.copy(); c=plan.conv_sq(current)
             # maybe break if too stuck
      if stuck > 30000: break
   return best.copy(), best_sc
```
This pair phase can be long. `rng.permutation(dono)` returns new. Good.
Potential bug: When multiplying `y=c + t*Larr`, `y` is new array. Modifying y doesn't affect c. Good.
If `best_t` equals hi and current[i] becomes tiny negative due t exact hi? cand[i] can be 0. If negative -1e-17, set 0; sum changes tiny, normalize. ok.
If p0/p1/p2 duplicate? no. If i=0,j=0 skip.
The `base_max` could be stale if c just recomputed after re; yes.
`best_v` initial base max. If some f gives exact equal not less by 1e-15, no accept. Good.
This pair phase recomputes `c` only on accept or re; but if no accept and c baseline unchanged, fine. If we perturb current at stuck, recompute c. Good.

But in the loop, after re computing at top and setting c, we then choose pair and use c. If no accept, c remains baseline. Good. If we accept, set c=plan.conv_sq(current) (normalized) but note `current` was normalized maybe sum exactly. Good.

#### optimize_candidate
```
def optimize_candidate(a, plan, limit, rng):
   current=a.copy()
   if time.monotonic() > limit: return current
   lp_dead=time.monotonic()
   remaining=limit-lp_dead
   if remaining>120:
      lp_limit=min(limit, lp_dead + max(30, remaining*0.35))
      current,_=lp_phase(current, plan, lp_limit, rng)
   # smooth
   remaining=limit-time.monotonic()
   if remaining>60:
      sm_limit=min(limit, time.monotonic() + max(20, remaining*0.45))
      current,_=smooth_phase(current, plan, sm_limit, rng)
   # pair
   if time.monotonic()<limit:
      current,_=pair_phase(current, plan, limit, rng)
   return current
```
But phases return best; current=best. Good.

### Exact update closure
```
best_score = np.inf; best_arr = None
def update_best(arr):
    nonlocal best_score,best_arr
    arr=normalize_array(arr)
    if arr is None: return
    sc=exact_score(arr)
    if sc < best_score:
        best_score=sc; best_arr=arr.copy()
```

### param_family
```
def param_family(p, n):
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   p=np.asarray(p)
   z = p[0]*x + p[1]*x*x + p[2]*np.cos(np.pi*x) + p[3]*np.sin(np.pi*x)
   if p.size>4: z += p[4]*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
   z -= np.max(z)
   z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 0.0)
   v=np.exp(z)
   s=v.sum()
   if s<1e-300: v=np.ones(n)/n; s=1
   return v/s
```
No p0; random length 4/5.

LP c_obj for peak-min: We minimize p, but we do not constrain p upper; okay. However if all constraints <=0, p=0 feasible? With sum g=1 and a nonnegative not all zero, cross coefficients nonnegative; p=0 only if cross all zero impossible. Good.

Peak-min LP objective may produce g concentrated to minimize max, but p perhaps very small. Good.

### Could there be no strict improvement due to local optimum but LP returns g with t>0 and same score within tolerance; stuck break. Good.

### Candidate add internal score uses `plan.conv_sq(arr).max()`; if arr has length but sum normalized, score =2*n*max. If arr sum normalization changed max? We normalize before.

### If generated N very small (parent size maybe 1), `ConvPlan` n=1, m=1, Ng=next_fast_len(1)=1. h slice? n-1=0, m=1. works. But optimization with n=1 trivial. We probably include N>=2. ### Possible performance issue: In pair phase, `c + t*Larr` creates a new array each eval and `y[p0]...` modifies. If c is large and t small, okay. We can reduce memory by using `np.multiply(Larr, t, out=?)` but no.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize


class ConvPlan:
    def __init__(self, n):
        self.n = int(n)
        self.m = 2 * self.n - 1
        self.N = next_fast_len(self.m)
        self.Ng = next_fast_len(3 * self.n - 2)

    def _sq(self, a):
        A = rfft(a, self.N)
        c = irfft(A * A, self.N)[:self.m]
        c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
        return c

    def conv_sq(self, a):
        return self._sq(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64))

    def conv2(self, u, v):
        U = rfft(np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64), self.N)
        V = rfft(np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64), self.N)
        c = irfft(U * V, self.N)[:self.m]
        c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
        return c

    def score(self, a):
        c = self.conv_sq(a)
        if c.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        return float(2.0 * self.n * np.max(c))

    def h_from_weights(self, a, w):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
        if w.size != self.m or a.size != self.n:
            return None
        Wr = w[::-1]
        A = rfft(a, self.Ng)
        W = rfft(Wr, self.Ng)
        t = irfft(A * W, self.Ng).real
        return t[self.n - 1:self.m][::-1]


def normalize_array(a, max_len=400):
    if a is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    if a.size > max_len:
        a = resample_array(a, min(max_len, 384))
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def resample_array(a, n):
    a = normalize_array(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.ones(max(2, int(n)), dtype=np.float64) / max(2, int(n))
    if a.size == int(n):
        return a
    old_x = (np.arange(a.size) + 0.5) / float(a.size)
    new_x = (np.arange(int(n)) + 0.5) / float(int(n))
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    b = normalize_array(b)
    if b is None:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / int(n)
    return b


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    if b.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        return float("inf")
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * mb / (s * s))


def softmax_weights(c, tau):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    x = np.clip((c - cm) / max(tau, 1e-300), -60.0, 0.0)
    ex = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    return ex / s


def active_weights(c, frac=0.2):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = c.size
    k = max(1, int(round(frac * m)))
    if k >= m:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, m - k)[m - k:]
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / len(idx)
    return w


def param_family(p, n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    while p.size < 4:
        p = np.concatenate([p, [0.0]])
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    z = p[0] * x + p[1] * x * x + p[2] * np.cos(np.pi * x) + p[3] * np.sin(np.pi * x)
    if p.size >= 5:
        z += p[4] * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 0.0)
    v = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(v))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        v = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        s = 1.0
    return v / s


def _best_blend_peak(c0, cross, cg):
    def vals_for_ts(ts):
        ts = np.asarray(ts, dtype=np.float64)
        o = 1.0 - ts
        arr = (
            (o * o)[:, None] * c0[None, :]
            + (2.0 * o * ts)[:, None] * cross[None, :]
            + (ts * ts)[:, None] * cg[None, :]
        )
        return np.max(arr, axis=1)

    ts = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 25)
    v = vals_for_ts(ts)
    i = int(np.argmin(v))
    if 0 < i < len(ts) - 1:
        lo, hi = float(ts[i - 1]), float(ts[i + 1])
    else:
        lo, hi = 0.0, 1.0
    ts2 = np.linspace(lo, hi, 25)
    v2 = vals_for_ts(ts2)
    j = int(np.argmin(v2))
    return float(ts2[j]), float(v2[j])


def _lp_phase(a, plan, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_array(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    current = a.copy()
    best = current.copy()
    cur_sc = plan.score(current)
    best_sc = cur_sc
    n = plan.n
    m = plan.m

    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj[-1] = 1.0
    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
    b_eq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(0.0, None)]
    b_ub0 = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj_sum = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds_g = [(0.0, None)] * n

    max_iters = 360 if n <= 210 else (240 if n <= 280 else 150)
    stuck = 0
    it = 0

    def try_g(g):
        g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if g.size != n:
            return None, float("inf")
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, 1000.0)
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs <= 1e-12:
            return None, float("inf")
        g = g / gs
        if float(np.linalg.norm(g - current, 1)) < 1e-8:
            return None, float("inf")
        c0 = plan.conv_sq(current)
        cross = plan.conv2(current, g)
        cg = plan.conv_sq(g)
        t, p = _best_blend_peak(c0, cross, cg)
        if t <= 1e-14:
            return None, float("inf")
        cand = (1.0 - t) * current + t * g
        cs = float(cand.sum())
        if cs <= 1e-12:
            return None, float("inf")
        cand = cand / cs
        return cand, 2.0 * n * p

    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        A = np.empty((m, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A[:, :n] = 0.0
        A[:, -1] = -1.0
        for j in range(n):
            A[j:j + n, j] = current
        B = A[:, :n]
        M = float(plan.conv_sq(current).max())
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
            break

        g1 = None
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b_ub0,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True},
            )
            if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                g1 = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            g1 = None

        cand = None
        cand_sc = float("inf")
        if g1 is not None:
            cand, cand_sc = try_g(g1)

        use_max = (it % 5 == 4) or (stuck >= 2)
        if use_max and M > 1e-300:
            try:
                res2 = linprog(
                    c_obj_sum,
                    A_ub=B,
                    b_ub=np.full(m, M, dtype=np.float64),
                    bounds=bounds_g,
                    method="highs",
                    options={"presolve": True},
                )
                if res2 is not None and getattr(res2, "x", None) is not None:
                    g2 = np.asarray(res2.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                    cand2, cand_sc2 = try_g(g2)
                    if cand2 is not None and cand_sc2 < cand_sc:
                        cand, cand_sc = cand2, cand_sc2
            except Exception:
                pass

        if cand is not None and cand_sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            current = cand
            best = cand.copy()
            best_sc = cand_sc
            stuck = 0
        else:
            stuck += 1
            if stuck > 10:
                break

    return best.copy(), best_sc


def _smooth_phase(a, plan, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_array(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    current = a.copy()
    best = current.copy()
    best_sc = plan.score(current)
    n = plan.n
    eta = 0.10
    stuck = 0
    max_iters = 6000 if n <= 220 else 3500

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        c = plan.conv_sq(current)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best = current.copy()
            best_sc = sc
            stuck = 0

        M = float(np.max(c))
        minc = float(np.min(c))
        if np.random.default_rng(0).random() < 0:  # placeholder, replaced below
            pass
        if rng.random() < 0.70:
            w = softmax_weights(c, max(1e-15, 0.045 * M))
        else:
            frac = 0.20 + 0.10 * (M - minc) / max(M, 1e-300)
            w = active_weights(c, frac)

        h = plan.h_from_weights(current, w)
        if h is None:
            break
        hm = float(np.max(h))
        if not np.isfinite(hm) or hm <= 1e-300:
            break
        hn = h / hm

        improved = False
        z = np.log(np.maximum(current, 1e-300))
        for e in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 2.0 * eta):
            z2 = z - e * hn
            z2 -= float(np.max(z2))
            cand = np.exp(z2)
            cs = float(cand.sum())
            if cs <= 1e-30:
                continue
            cand /= cs
            csc = plan.score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(csc) and csc < sc - 1e-13:
                current = cand
                sc = csc
                best = current.copy()
                best_sc = sc
                eta = min(2.0, max(0.01, e * 1.05))
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            step = hn - float(np.dot(hn, current))
            sn = float(np.linalg.norm(step))
            if sn > 1e-14:
                for alpha in (0.02 * eta, 0.05 * eta, 0.10 * eta):
                    cand = current - alpha * step / sn
                    cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                    cs = float(cand.sum())
                    if cs <= 1e-30:
                        continue
                    cand /= cs
                    csc = plan.score(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(csc) and csc < sc - 1e-13:
                        current = cand
                        sc = csc
                        best = current.copy()
                        best_sc = sc
                        eta = min(2.0, max(0.01, alpha * 5.0))
                        improved = True
                        break

        if not improved:
            stuck += 1
            eta = max(0.015, eta * 0.90)
            if stuck > 350:
                break
            if stuck % 35 == 0 and stuck < 350:
                noised = normalize_array(current + rng.normal(0.0, 0.008, current.size))
                if noised is not None:
                    sn_score = plan.score(noised)
                    if sn_score < best_sc + max(1e-8, 5e-5 * best_sc):
                        current = noised
                    else:
                        current = best.copy()

    return best.copy(), best_sc


def _pair_phase(a, plan, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_array(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    current = a.copy()
    best = current.copy()
    best_sc = plan.score(current)
    c = plan.conv_sq(current)
    cur_sc = float(2.0 * plan.n * np.max(c))
    best_sc = min(best_sc, cur_sc)
    n = plan.n
    m = plan.m

    fracs = (0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20, 0.30, 0.40, 0.55, 0.70, 0.85, 1.00)
    dono = None
    reci = None
    recompute = 0
    stuck = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if recompute <= 0 or dono is None:
            c = plan.conv_sq(current)
            cur_sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
            if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                best = current.copy()
                best_sc = cur_sc

            M = float(np.max(c))
            if M <= 1e-300:
                break
            if rng.random() < 0.55:
                w = softmax_weights(c, max(1e-15, 0.05 * M))
            else:
                w = active_weights(c, 0.18)
            h = plan.h_from_weights(current, w)
            if h is None:
                break
            hord = np.argsort(h)
            L = max(4, n // 4)
            dono = hord[-L:].copy()
            reci = hord[:L].copy()
            dono = rng.permutation(dono)
            reci = rng.permutation(reci)
            recompute = 35
        recompute -= 1

        i = int(rng.choice(dono))
        j = int(rng.choice(reci))
        if i == j:
            continue
        hi = float(current[i])
        if hi <= 1e-15:
            continue

        Larr = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        Larr[j:j + n] = current
        Larr[i:i + n] -= current
        Larr *= 2.0
        p0 = 2 * i
        p1 = i + j
        p2 = 2 * j
        base = float(np.max(c))

        best_t = 0.0
        best_v = base

        for f in fracs:
            t = f * hi
            y = c + t * Larr
            t2 = t * t
            y[p0] += t2
            y[p1] -= 2.0 * t2
            y[p2] += t2
            v = float(np.max(y))
            if v < best_v - 1e-16:
                best_v = v
                best_t = t

        if best_t > 0.0:
            f0 = best_t / hi
            lo = max(0.0, f0 - 0.10)
            hi2 = min(1.0, f0 + 0.10)
            if hi2 > lo:
                for f in np.linspace(lo, hi2, 13):
                    if f <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    t = f * hi
                    y = c + t * Larr
                    t2 = t * t
                    y[p0] += t2
                    y[p1] -= 2.0 * t2
                    y[p2] += t2
                    v = float(np.max(y))
                    if v < best_v - 1e-16:
                        best_v = v
                        best_t = t

        if best_t > 0.0:
            cand = current.copy()
            cand[i] -= best_t
            cand[j] += best_t
            if cand[i] < 0.0:
                cand[i] = 0.0
            s = float(cand.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300:
                stuck += 1
            else:
                cand /= s
                c2 = plan.conv_sq(cand)
                sc2 = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c2))
                if sc2 < cur_sc - 1e-14:
                    current = cand
                    c = c2
                    cur_sc = sc2
                    recompute = 20
                    stuck = 0
                    if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
                        best = current.copy()
                        best_sc = sc2
                else:
                    stuck += 1
        else:
            stuck += 1

        if stuck > 0 and stuck % 5000 == 0:
            noised = normalize_array(best + rng.normal(0.0, 0.004, best.size))
            if noised is None:
                break
            sn = plan.score(noised)
            allow = best_sc + (0.001 if stuck > 20000 else 1e-7)
            if np.isfinite(sn) and sn < allow:
                current = noised
                c = plan.conv_sq(current)
                cur_sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
            else:
                current = best.copy()
                c = plan.conv_sq(current)
                cur_sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))

        if stuck > 40000:
            break

    return best.copy(), best_sc


def _optimize_candidate(a, plan, limit, rng):
    current = normalize_array(a)
    if current is None:
        return a
    if time.monotonic() >= limit:
        return current

    remaining = max(0.0, limit - time.monotonic())
    if remaining > 120.0:
        lp_limit = min(limit, time.monotonic() + max(25.0, 0.33 * remaining))
        out, _ = _lp_phase(current, plan, lp_limit, rng)
        if out is not None:
            current = out

    remaining = max(0.0, limit - time.monotonic())
    if remaining > 50.0:
        sm_limit = min(limit, time.monotonic() + max(15.0, 0.45 * remaining))
        out, _ = _smooth_phase(current, plan, sm_limit, rng)
        if out is not None:
            current = out

    if time.monotonic() < limit:
        out, _ = _pair_phase(current, plan, limit, rng)
        if out is not None:
            current = out

    return current


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget - 10.0

    try:
        parent = normalize_array(height_sequence_1)
    except Exception:
        parent = None
    if parent is None:
        parent = np.ones(192, dtype=np.float64) / 192.0

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_arr = None

    def update_best(arr):
        nonlocal best_score, best_arr
        arr = normalize_array(arr)
        if arr is None:
            return
        sc = exact_score(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_arr = arr.copy()

    update_best(parent)

    plan_cache = {}

    def get_plan(n):
        n = int(n)
        if n not in plan_cache:
            plan_cache[n] = ConvPlan(n)
        return plan_cache[n]

    gen_budget = min(80.0, max(20.0, 0.06 * budget))
    gen_deadline = min(deadline - max(60.0, 0.15 * budget), t0 + gen_budget)

    cands = []

    def add_candidate(arr, tag):
        if time.monotonic() > gen_deadline:
            return
        arr = normalize_array(arr)
        if arr is None:
            return
        n = arr.size
        if not (2 <= n <= 384):
            arr = resample_array(arr, 192)
            if arr is None:
                return
            n = arr.size
        plan = get_plan(n)
        try:
            sc = plan.score(arr)
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            cands.append((sc, arr.copy(), n, tag))

    parent = resample_array(parent, parent.size)
    add_candidate(parent, "parent")

    lens = [192, 256]
    if budget > 1000.0:
        lens.append(320)
    parent_n = parent.size
    if parent_n not in lens and 16 <= parent_n <= 320:
        lens.append(parent_n)
    lens = list(dict.fromkeys(lens))

    rng_init = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Resampled parent at several lengths.
    for N in lens:
        if time.monotonic() > gen_deadline:
            break
        if N == parent_n:
            add_candidate(parent, f"parent{N}")
        else:
            add_candidate(resample_array(parent, N), f"resample{N}")

    # Compact power-law initializations.
    for N in lens:
        x = (np.arange(N) + 0.5) / float(N)
        exs = (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0)
        for ae in exs:
            for be in exs:
                if time.monotonic() > gen_deadline:
                    break
                v = x ** ae * (1.0 - x) ** be
                add_candidate(v, f"power{N}")

    # Low-dimensional parametric initializations + a quick local refinement.
    per_len = max(70, int(520 / max(1, len(lens))))
    for N in lens:
        if time.monotonic() > gen_deadline:
            break
        plan = get_plan(N)
        best_p = None
        best_ps = float("inf")
        for _ in range(per_len):
            if time.monotonic() > gen_deadline:
                break
            p = rng_init.normal(0.0, 1.0, 4)
            arr = param_family(p, N)
            add_candidate(arr, f"param{N}")
            try:
                sc = 2.0 * N * float(np.max(plan.conv_sq(arr)))
            except Exception:
                sc = float("inf")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_ps:
                best_ps = sc
                best_p = p.copy()

        if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < gen_deadline - 20.0:
            def obj(q, N=N, plan=plan):
                try:
                    arr = param_family(q, N)
                    return 2.0 * N * float(np.max(plan.conv_sq(arr)))
                except Exception:
                    return 1e6

            try:
                res = minimize(
                    obj,
                    best_p,
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={"maxfev": 120, "adaptive": True, "xatol": 1e-3, "fatol": 1e-5},
                )
                if res.x is not None:
                    add_candidate(param_family(res.x, N), f"nn{N}")
            except Exception:
                pass

    if not cands:
        cands.append((exact_score(parent), parent.copy(), parent.size, "fallback_parent"))

    cands.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])

    selected = []
    parent_item = None
    for item in cands:
        if item[3] == "parent" and parent_item is None:
            parent_item = item
            break
    if parent_item is not None:
        selected.append(parent_item)

    threshold = (parent_item[0] if parent_item is not None else cands[0][0]) + 0.06
    for item in cands:
        if len(selected) >= 4:
            break
        if item is parent_item:
            continue
        if item[0] <= threshold:
            selected.append(item)

    if len(selected) < 2:
        for item in cands:
            if item not in selected:
                selected.append(item)
            if len(selected) >= 3:
                break

    selected.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])

    for item in selected:
        update_best(item[1])

    rng_main = np.random.default_rng(seed + 101)
    fracs_alloc = (0.55, 0.30, 0.12, 0.03)

    for idx, item in enumerate(selected):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 45.0:
            break
        remaining = deadline - now - 25.0
        if remaining < 50.0:
            break
        frac = fracs_alloc[idx] if idx < len(fracs_alloc) else 0.03
        alloc = max(70.0, frac * remaining)
        limit = min(deadline - 12.0, now + alloc)
        N = item[2]
        plan = get_plan(N)
        out = _optimize_candidate(item[1], plan, limit, rng_main)
        update_best(out)

    # Final exact polishing of the current best candidate.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if best_arr is not None and now < deadline - 35.0:
        remaining = deadline - now - 10.0
        if remaining > 40.0:
            N = best_arr.size
            plan = get_plan(N)
            rng_pol = np.random.default_rng(seed + 909)
            out, _ = _pair_phase(best_arr.copy(), plan, now + remaining, rng_pol)
            if out is not None:
                update_best(out)

    if best_arr is None:
        best_arr = np.ones(192, dtype=np.float64) / 192.0

    try:
        best_arr = np.clip(np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), 0.0, 1000.0)
        if best_arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_arr)):
            best_arr = np.ones(192, dtype=np.float64) / 192.0
        s = float(best_arr.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            best_arr = np.ones(192, dtype=np.float64) / 192.0
            s = 1.0
        best_arr = best_arr / s
        ans = [float(x) for x in best_arr]
    except Exception:
        ans = [1.0 / 192.0] * 192

    try:
        fs = float(evaluate_sequence(ans))
    except Exception:
        fs = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(fs):
        ans = [1.0 / 192.0] * 192
        try:
            fs = float(evaluate_sequence(ans))
        except Exception:
            pass
        if not np.isfinite(fs):
            ans = [1.0] * 192

    return ans
```
